corelane-cluster.html:
```html
<!doctype html>
<!--
  INSTALANDING.AI · Corelane Build Cluster Page
  slug     corelane-build-cluster-page
  prompt   https://instalanding.ai/p/corelane-build-cluster-page.txt   (the one-shot brief this file was built from)
  page     https://instalanding.ai/prompt/corelane-build-cluster-page
  license  Fictional product; replace names, copy, prices and contact details before shipping.

  MAP
  tokens    :root at the top: --night #0f054c (page), --indigo #24126a, --ring #706ec4 (pill outlines), --lavender
            #e9e9fd (text, filled buttons), --cyan #42e8e0 (available, focus), --magenta #e84298 (building),
            --display Oswald, --text Instrument Sans, --wrap 1200px
  sections  nav, hero, product, fleet, how, pricing, faq, cta, footer (data-section)
  scripts   no libraries. Script 1: the hero scene, an isometric illustration exported from a vector-animation file and
            replayed by a tiny runtime (D = nodes, colour keys, morphing paths, keyframed animations; a 4-layer state
            machine picks "laptops" or "server" from the segmented control and lifts the core server on click).
            Script 2: header scroll state + mobile menu + scroll-spy, the 96-machine fleet board (click to sleep or
            wake, Run a build spreads work out from the centre), the monthly/yearly pricing switch, the trial form
  assets    none: every machine, server and circuit field is inline SVG; fonts from Google Fonts

  ADAPTING
  - The hero art is data-driven: element ids (n1768, p14874...) are referenced by D in script 1. Edit colours in the
    SVG freely, but keep those ids or the animation silently stops touching them.
  - The circuit field is masked on all four edges and the core field only animates while "Core server" is selected;
    keep both inside .stage so the glow swap (data-scene) still works.
  - Fleet speed-up is SINGLE / (1 + 0.16 * (n - 1)): change the 0.16 to make the cluster feel faster or slower.
  - Wire the trial form to a real endpoint; it only validates and shows a message today.
-->
<html lang="en">
<head>
<meta charset="utf-8">
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1">
<title>Corelane — your team's laptops, one build cluster</title>
<meta name="description" content="Corelane pools the idle cores in your team's laptops with one on-site server, so builds and tests finish in minutes on hardware you already own.">
<link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.googleapis.com">
<link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.gstatic.com" crossorigin>
<link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Oswald:wght@400;500&family=Instrument+Sans:wght@400;500;600&display=swap" rel="stylesheet">
<style>
  :root {
    --night: #0f054c;
    --night-2: #160a5c;
    --indigo: #24126a;
    --ring: #706ec4;
    --ring-soft: rgba(112, 110, 196, .32);
    --lavender: #e9e9fd;
    --muted: #b6b3e3;
    --faint: #8a86c9;
    --cyan: #42e8e0;
    --magenta: #e84298;
    --display: "Oswald", "Arial Narrow", sans-serif;
    --text: "Instrument Sans", system-ui, sans-serif;
    --wrap: 1200px;
    --gutter: clamp(20px, 4vw, 40px);
  }
  *, *::before, *::after { box-sizing: border-box; }
  * { margin: 0; }
  html { scroll-behavior: smooth; scroll-padding-top: 84px; -webkit-text-size-adjust: 100%; }
  body {
    background: var(--night); color: var(--lavender);
    font: 400 17px/1.6 var(--text);
    -webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased; text-rendering: optimizeLegibility;
    overflow-x: hidden;
  }
  img, svg { display: block; max-width: 100%; }
  a { color: inherit; }
  button, input { font: inherit; color: inherit; }
  :focus-visible { outline: 2px solid var(--cyan); outline-offset: 3px; border-radius: 4px; }
  .wrap { width: min(var(--wrap), 100% - 2 * var(--gutter)); margin-inline: auto; }
  .sr-only { position: absolute; width: 1px; height: 1px; overflow: hidden; clip: rect(0 0 0 0); white-space: nowrap; }

  /* ---------- type ---------- */
  h1, h2, h3 { font-family: var(--display); font-weight: 500; line-height: 1.05; letter-spacing: -.005em; }
  h2 { font-size: clamp(2.1rem, 4.2vw, 3.5rem); text-wrap: balance; }
  h3 { font-size: 1.45rem; letter-spacing: 0; line-height: 1.2; }
  .lead { font-size: clamp(1.05rem, 1.4vw, 1.2rem); color: var(--muted); max-width: 34em; }
  p { text-wrap: pretty; }

  /* ---------- buttons (pill + ring, from the original artwork) ---------- */
  .btn {
    --h: 48px;
    display: inline-flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; gap: .5em;
    height: var(--h); padding: 0 1.6em; border-radius: 999px;
    font: 500 1rem/1 var(--display); letter-spacing: .04em; text-transform: uppercase;
    text-decoration: none; cursor: pointer; white-space: nowrap;
    border: 1.5px solid var(--ring); background: transparent;
    transition: background-color .2s, border-color .2s, color .2s, transform .2s;
  }
  .btn:hover { background: rgba(112, 110, 196, .18); }
  .btn:active { transform: scale(.98); }
  .btn-fill { background: var(--lavender); color: var(--night); border-color: var(--lavender); }
  .btn-fill:hover { background: #fff; border-color: #fff; }
  .btn-sm { --h: 38px; padding: 0 1.2em; font-size: .9rem; }

  /* ---------- header ---------- */
  .site-header {
    position: fixed; inset: 0 0 auto; z-index: 50;
    transition: background-color .3s, border-color .3s, backdrop-filter .3s;
    border-bottom: 1px solid transparent;
  }
  .site-header.is-scrolled {
    background: rgba(15, 5, 76, .78); border-bottom-color: rgba(112, 110, 196, .22);
    -webkit-backdrop-filter: blur(14px) saturate(140%); backdrop-filter: blur(14px) saturate(140%);
  }
  .bar { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 28px; height: 76px; }
  .brand { display: inline-flex; align-items: center; gap: 10px; text-decoration: none; font: 500 1.45rem/1 var(--display); letter-spacing: .01em; }
  .brand-mark { width: 22px; height: 22px; display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(3, 1fr); gap: 2px; transform: rotate(45deg) scale(.85); }
  .brand-mark i { border-radius: 1px; background: var(--cyan); }
  .brand-mark i:nth-child(3n+2) { background: var(--lavender); }
  .brand-mark i:nth-child(5) { background: var(--magenta); }
  .menu { display: contents; }
  .nav { display: flex; gap: 30px; margin-left: auto; }
  .nav a { text-decoration: none; color: var(--muted); font-size: .95rem; font-weight: 500; transition: color .2s; position: relative; }
  .nav a:hover { color: var(--lavender); }
  .nav a::after { content: ""; position: absolute; left: 0; right: 0; bottom: -6px; height: 1.5px; background: var(--lavender); transform: scaleX(0); transform-origin: left; transition: transform .25s; }
  .nav a:hover::after, .nav a.is-current::after { transform: scaleX(1); }
  .nav a.is-current { color: var(--lavender); }
  .bar-actions { display: flex; gap: 10px; }
  .menu-btn { display: none; margin-left: auto; width: 44px; height: 44px; border-radius: 50%; border: 1.5px solid var(--ring); background: none; cursor: pointer; }
  .menu-btn span { display: block; width: 16px; height: 1.5px; margin: 4px auto; background: var(--lavender); transition: transform .25s; }
  .menu-btn[aria-expanded="true"] span:first-child { transform: translateY(2.75px) rotate(45deg); }
  .menu-btn[aria-expanded="true"] span:last-child { transform: translateY(-2.75px) rotate(-45deg); }

  /* ---------- hero ---------- */
  .hero { position: relative; overflow: hidden; padding: 128px 0 48px; isolation: isolate; }
  .hero::after { /* fade the circuit field into the next section */
    content: ""; position: absolute; inset: auto 0 0; height: 160px; z-index: 1; pointer-events: none;
    background: linear-gradient(to bottom, transparent, var(--night));
  }
  .hero-grid { position: relative; z-index: 2; display: grid; grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 5fr) minmax(0, 7fr); align-items: center; gap: 24px; }
  .hero-copy { position: relative; z-index: 3; padding-block: 40px; }
  .hero h1 {
    font-size: clamp(2.7rem, 5.6vw, 5rem); line-height: .96; text-transform: uppercase; letter-spacing: -.012em;
    max-width: 9.5em;
  }
  .hero .lead { margin-top: 26px; }
  .hero-ctas { display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 12px; margin-top: 36px; }
  .hero-note { margin-top: 18px; font-size: .9rem; color: var(--faint); }

  .stage { position: relative; aspect-ratio: 400 / 320; }
  .stage > svg { position: absolute; inset: 0; width: 100%; height: 100%; overflow: visible; pointer-events: none; z-index: 1; }
  .stage::before, .stage::after { /* glow behind the illustration: violet for machines, teal for the core */
    content: ""; position: absolute; left: -30%; top: -40%; width: 110%; height: 160%; pointer-events: none; z-index: 0;
    background: radial-gradient(closest-side, rgba(87, 97, 215, .62), rgba(62, 65, 166, .38) 30%, rgba(45, 43, 133, .18) 60%, transparent 100%);
    transition: opacity .7s ease;
  }
  .stage::after {
    left: -18%; top: -20%; width: 100%; height: 150%; opacity: 0;
    background: radial-gradient(closest-side, rgba(66, 232, 224, .30), rgba(48, 120, 190, .22) 35%, rgba(36, 18, 106, .12) 65%, transparent 100%);
  }
  .stage[data-scene="2"]::before { opacity: .55; }
  .stage[data-scene="2"]::after { opacity: 1; }

  /* background fields share the original 600x400 frame, aligned to the artwork */
  .stage > svg.field { inset: auto; left: -50%; top: -18.75%; width: 150%; max-width: none; height: 125%; transition: opacity .7s ease; }
  .stage > svg.field.circuits { width: 225%; } /* 900-unit frame: original lines plus a mirrored run to the right */
  .stage > svg.circuits {
    /* feather all four edges so the lines run behind and around the machines, then dissolve */
    -webkit-mask-image: linear-gradient(to right, transparent 1%, #000 20%, #000 86%, transparent 100%), linear-gradient(to bottom, transparent 2%, #000 13%, #000 80%, transparent 97%);
    -webkit-mask-composite: source-in;
    mask-image: linear-gradient(to right, transparent 1%, #000 20%, #000 86%, transparent 100%), linear-gradient(to bottom, transparent 2%, #000 13%, #000 80%, transparent 97%);
    mask-composite: intersect;
  }
  .circuits .flow { mix-blend-mode: screen; filter: drop-shadow(0 0 2px rgba(66, 232, 224, .9)); }
  .core-field { opacity: 0; }
  .stage[data-scene="2"] .circuits { opacity: 0; }
  .stage[data-scene="2"] .core-field { opacity: 1; }
  .core-field .ring { stroke-width: 1.3; opacity: 0; transform-box: fill-box; transform-origin: center; animation: ripple 4.4s cubic-bezier(.25, .6, .35, 1) var(--d) infinite; }
  .core-field .tile { opacity: 0; animation: blink 4.4s ease-in-out var(--d) infinite; }
  .core-field .mote { opacity: 0; transform-box: fill-box; animation: rise 5.6s ease-in var(--d) infinite; }
  .stage:not([data-scene="2"]) .core-field * { animation-play-state: paused; }
  @keyframes ripple { 0% { transform: scale(.9); opacity: 0; } 12% { opacity: .9; } 100% { transform: scale(3.6); opacity: 0; } }
  @keyframes blink { 0%, 100% { opacity: 0; } 45% { opacity: .38; } }
  @keyframes rise { 0% { transform: translateY(0); opacity: 0; } 15% { opacity: .9; } 100% { transform: translateY(-80px); opacity: 0; } }
  @media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
    .core-field .ring, .core-field .tile, .core-field .mote { animation: none; }
    .core-field .ring:nth-child(1) { opacity: .7; transform: scale(1.4); }
    .core-field .ring:nth-child(2) { opacity: .35; transform: scale(2.3); }
    .core-field .tile { opacity: .25; }
  }
  .hero-art { transition: opacity .22s ease, transform .22s ease; }
  .hero-art.is-swapping { opacity: 0; transform: scale(.985); }
  #n1768 { pointer-events: visiblePainted; cursor: pointer; outline: none; }
  #n1768:focus-visible { filter: drop-shadow(0 0 6px var(--cyan)); }

  .art-controls { position: relative; z-index: 3; display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 14px 18px; margin-top: 4px; }
  .segmented { display: inline-flex; padding: 4px; border-radius: 999px; border: 1px solid var(--ring-soft); background: rgba(15, 5, 76, .55); -webkit-backdrop-filter: blur(8px); backdrop-filter: blur(8px); }
  .segmented button { border: 0; background: none; cursor: pointer; padding: 8px 16px; border-radius: 999px; font-size: .9rem; font-weight: 500; color: var(--muted); transition: background-color .2s, color .2s; }
  .segmented button[aria-pressed="true"] { background: var(--lavender); color: var(--night); }
  .art-hint { font-size: .85rem; color: var(--faint); }

  /* ---------- sections ---------- */
  section { position: relative; }
  .section { padding-block: clamp(72px, 8vw, 112px); }
  .section-head { display: grid; grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr) minmax(0, 1fr); gap: 24px 60px; align-items: end; margin-bottom: clamp(44px, 6vw, 72px); }
  .section-head .lead { justify-self: start; }

  /* capabilities: a ledger, not a card grid */
  .ledger { display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr)); border-top: 1px solid var(--ring-soft); }
  .ledger article { padding: 34px 40px 40px 0; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--ring-soft); display: grid; grid-template-columns: 54px 1fr; gap: 0 18px; }
  .ledger article:nth-child(even) { padding-left: 40px; padding-right: 0; border-left: 1px solid var(--ring-soft); }
  .ledger .glyph { width: 46px; height: 46px; margin-top: 2px; }
  .ledger h3 { margin-bottom: 8px; }
  .ledger p { color: var(--muted); max-width: 32em; }

  /* ---------- fleet demo (the centerpiece) ---------- */
  .fleet { background: radial-gradient(60% 70% at 70% 50%, #2a1a7e 0%, rgba(36, 18, 106, .55) 40%, transparent 75%), var(--night); }
  .fleet-grid { display: grid; grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 5fr) minmax(0, 7fr); gap: 48px; align-items: center; }
  .fleet-copy .lead { margin-top: 20px; }
  .readout { margin-top: 36px; display: grid; gap: 0; border-top: 1px solid var(--ring-soft); }
  .readout div { display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: baseline; gap: 16px; padding: 14px 0; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--ring-soft); }
  .readout dt { color: var(--muted); font-size: .95rem; }
  .readout dd { font: 500 1.6rem/1 var(--display); font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums; }
  .readout dd small { font: 400 .9rem var(--text); color: var(--faint); margin-left: 6px; }
  .fleet-actions { display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; align-items: center; gap: 14px 20px; margin-top: 28px; }
  .legend { display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 6px 16px; font-size: .85rem; color: var(--muted); list-style: none; padding: 0; }
  .legend li { display: inline-flex; align-items: center; gap: 7px; }
  .legend i { width: 9px; height: 9px; border-radius: 2px; transform: rotate(45deg); }

  .board { position: relative; aspect-ratio: 1 / .78; display: grid; place-items: center; perspective: 1800px; }
  .plate {
    --cell: clamp(15px, 2.3vw, 27px);
    position: relative; padding: calc(var(--cell) * .9); border-radius: 18px;
    background: linear-gradient(135deg, #e6e4ff 0%, #b9b5f5 100%);
    transform: rotateX(57deg) rotateZ(45deg);
    box-shadow:
      1px 1px 0 #8f8ae0, 2px 2px 0 #8f8ae0, 3px 3px 0 #8580da, 4px 4px 0 #7d78d4, 5px 5px 0 #7570cf,
      6px 6px 0 #6d68ca, 7px 7px 0 #6560c4, 8px 8px 0 #5d58be, 9px 9px 0 #5550b8, 10px 10px 0 #4d48b2,
      30px 30px 50px rgba(5, 0, 30, .55), 0 0 80px rgba(66, 232, 224, .12);
  }
  .well {
    display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(12, var(--cell)); gap: calc(var(--cell) * .42);
    padding: calc(var(--cell) * .6); border-radius: 10px;
    background: #2c1d86; box-shadow: inset 2px 2px 0 rgba(0, 0, 0, .25), inset -1px -1px 0 rgba(255, 255, 255, .15);
  }
  .cell {
    width: var(--cell); aspect-ratio: 1; padding: 0; border: 0; border-radius: 3px; cursor: pointer;
    background: var(--cyan); box-shadow: 0 0 10px rgba(66, 232, 224, .55);
    transition: background-color .35s, box-shadow .35s, transform .2s;
  }
  .cell:hover { transform: translate(-2px, -2px); }
  .cell.busy { background: var(--magenta); box-shadow: 0 0 12px rgba(232, 66, 152, .75); }
  .cell.asleep { background: #4a3ca0; box-shadow: none; }
  .cell:focus-visible { outline: 2px solid #fff; outline-offset: 2px; }
  .core-chip {
    position: absolute; left: 50%; bottom: 4%; transform: translateX(-50%);
    font-size: .85rem; color: var(--muted); white-space: nowrap;
  }
  .demo-tag { position: absolute; top: 2%; right: 0; font-size: .8rem; color: var(--faint); border: 1px solid var(--ring-soft); padding: 4px 10px; border-radius: 999px; }

  /* ---------- how it works ---------- */
  .steps { list-style: none; padding: 0; display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(3, minmax(0, 1fr)); gap: 0; counter-reset: step; }
  .steps li { position: relative; padding: 0 36px; border-left: 1px solid var(--ring-soft); counter-increment: step; }
  .steps li:first-child { padding-left: 0; border-left: 0; }
  .steps .art { height: 190px; display: flex; align-items: flex-end; margin-bottom: 30px; }
  .steps .art svg { height: 100%; width: auto; max-width: 100%; }
  .steps h3::before { content: counter(step); display: block; font: 400 .95rem var(--display); color: var(--cyan); margin-bottom: 10px; }
  .steps p { color: var(--muted); margin-top: 10px; }
  .steps code { font: 500 .88em ui-monospace, SFMono-Regular, Menlo, monospace; background: rgba(112, 110, 196, .2); padding: 2px 7px; border-radius: 6px; color: var(--lavender); }

  /* ---------- pricing ---------- */
  .pricing { background: linear-gradient(to bottom, var(--night), var(--night-2) 40%, var(--night)); }
  .billing { display: inline-flex; align-items: center; gap: 12px; margin-top: 28px; font-size: .95rem; color: var(--muted); }
  .switch { position: relative; width: 50px; height: 28px; border-radius: 999px; border: 1.5px solid var(--ring); background: none; cursor: pointer; padding: 0; }
  .switch::after { content: ""; position: absolute; top: 3px; left: 3px; width: 19px; height: 19px; border-radius: 50%; background: var(--lavender); transition: transform .22s; }
  .switch[aria-checked="true"] { background: rgba(112, 110, 196, .35); }
  .switch[aria-checked="true"]::after { transform: translateX(22px); }
  .save { color: var(--cyan); }
  .plans { display: grid; grid-template-columns: 1fr 1.12fr 1fr; align-items: stretch; border: 1px solid var(--ring-soft); border-radius: 22px; overflow: hidden; }
  .plan { padding: 40px 36px 42px; display: flex; flex-direction: column; }
  .plan + .plan { border-left: 1px solid var(--ring-soft); }
  .plan.featured { background: var(--lavender); color: var(--night); }
  .plan.featured .plan-desc, .plan.featured .unit, .plan.featured li { color: #3b3180; }
  .plan.featured li::before { background: var(--magenta); }
  .plan h3 { font-size: 1.6rem; display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center; gap: 12px; }
  .badge { font: 500 .78rem var(--text); padding: 4px 10px; border-radius: 999px; background: var(--night); color: var(--lavender); }
  .plan-desc { color: var(--muted); margin-top: 10px; min-height: 3.2em; }
  .price { margin-top: 26px; font: 500 3.6rem/1 var(--display); font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums; display: flex; align-items: baseline; gap: 8px; }
  .unit { font: 400 .92rem/1.35 var(--text); color: var(--faint); margin-top: 12px; }
  .plan ul { list-style: none; padding: 0; margin: 28px 0 34px; display: grid; gap: 11px; font-size: .97rem; color: var(--muted); }
  .plan li { display: flex; gap: 12px; align-items: baseline; }
  .plan li::before { content: ""; flex: none; width: 7px; height: 7px; border-radius: 1.5px; background: var(--cyan); transform: rotate(45deg) translateY(-1px); }
  .plan .btn { margin-top: auto; width: 100%; }
  .plan.featured .btn { background: var(--night); border-color: var(--night); color: var(--lavender); }
  .plan.featured .btn:hover { background: var(--indigo); }
  .plan.featured :focus-visible { outline-color: var(--night); }

  /* ---------- faq ---------- */
  .faq-grid { display: grid; grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 4fr) minmax(0, 7fr); gap: 60px; align-items: start; }
  .faq-grid .lead { margin-top: 20px; }
  .faq-grid .lead a { color: var(--lavender); text-underline-offset: 3px; }
  .faq details { border-bottom: 1px solid var(--ring-soft); }
  .faq details:first-child { border-top: 1px solid var(--ring-soft); }
  .faq summary { list-style: none; cursor: pointer; padding: 24px 44px 24px 0; position: relative; font: 500 1.3rem/1.3 var(--display); }
  .faq summary::-webkit-details-marker { display: none; }
  .faq summary::after { content: ""; position: absolute; right: 6px; top: 50%; width: 12px; height: 12px; margin-top: -8px; border-right: 1.5px solid var(--ring); border-bottom: 1.5px solid var(--ring); transform: rotate(45deg); transition: transform .25s; }
  .faq details[open] summary::after { transform: rotate(225deg); margin-top: -2px; }
  .faq details p { color: var(--muted); padding: 0 44px 26px 0; max-width: 40em; }

  /* ---------- closing cta ---------- */
  .closing { padding-block: 40px clamp(80px, 10vw, 130px); }
  .closing-card {
    position: relative; overflow: hidden; border-radius: 28px; padding: clamp(40px, 6vw, 80px);
    background: radial-gradient(80% 120% at 85% 30%, #3e41a6 0%, #24126a 45%, #160a5c 100%);
    display: grid; grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 7fr) minmax(0, 5fr); gap: 40px; align-items: center;
    border: 1px solid var(--ring-soft);
  }
  .closing-card h2 { font-size: clamp(2rem, 3.8vw, 3.2rem); max-width: 14em; position: relative; z-index: 1; }
  .closing-card .lead { margin-top: 18px; position: relative; z-index: 1; }
  .signup { position: relative; z-index: 1; margin-top: 30px; display: flex; gap: 10px; max-width: 480px; }
  .signup input {
    flex: 1; min-width: 0; height: 48px; padding: 0 20px; border-radius: 999px;
    border: 1.5px solid var(--ring); background: rgba(15, 5, 76, .5); color: var(--lavender);
  }
  .signup input::placeholder { color: var(--faint); }
  .signup input:focus-visible { outline: 2px solid var(--cyan); outline-offset: 2px; }
  .form-msg { position: relative; z-index: 1; margin-top: 12px; font-size: .92rem; color: var(--muted); min-height: 1.5em; }
  .form-msg.ok { color: var(--cyan); }
  .form-msg.err { color: #ff9cc9; }
  .closing-art { position: relative; justify-self: center; width: min(100%, 340px); }
  .closing-art svg { width: 100%; height: auto; filter: drop-shadow(0 30px 40px rgba(5, 0, 30, .5)); }

  /* ---------- footer ---------- */
  .site-footer { border-top: 1px solid var(--ring-soft); padding: 64px 0 40px; font-size: .95rem; }
  .foot-grid { display: grid; grid-template-columns: 2fr repeat(3, 1fr); gap: 40px; }
  .foot-grid p { color: var(--faint); margin-top: 14px; max-width: 26em; }
  .foot-grid h3 { font: 500 1rem var(--display); letter-spacing: .02em; margin-bottom: 14px; }
  .foot-grid ul { list-style: none; padding: 0; display: grid; gap: 9px; }
  .foot-grid a { color: var(--muted); text-decoration: none; }
  .foot-grid a:hover { color: var(--lavender); text-decoration: underline; text-underline-offset: 3px; }
  .legal { margin-top: 56px; display: flex; justify-content: space-between; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 12px; color: var(--faint); font-size: .88rem; }

  /* ---------- single orchestrated entrance (hero only) ---------- */
  @media (prefers-reduced-motion: no-preference) {
    .hero h1, .hero .lead, .hero-ctas, .hero-note { animation: rise .9s cubic-bezier(.2, .7, .2, 1) both; }
    .hero .lead { animation-delay: .12s; }
    .hero-ctas { animation-delay: .22s; }
    .hero-note { animation-delay: .3s; }
    .hero .stage { animation: settle 1.3s cubic-bezier(.2, .7, .2, 1) .1s both; }
    @keyframes rise { from { opacity: 0; transform: translateY(18px); } }
    @keyframes settle { from { opacity: 0; transform: translateY(30px) scale(.97); } }
  }
  @media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
    html { scroll-behavior: auto; }
    *, *::before, *::after { transition-duration: .01ms !important; }
  }

  /* ---------- responsive ---------- */
  @media (max-width: 1080px) {
    .bar-actions .btn:not(.btn-fill) { display: none; }
    .nav { gap: 22px; }
  }
  @media (max-width: 960px) {
    .hero { padding-top: 112px; }
    .hero-grid, .fleet-grid, .faq-grid, .closing-card, .section-head { grid-template-columns: 1fr; }
    .hero-copy { padding-block: 0 8px; }
    .stage { margin-inline: -4%; }
    .fleet-grid { gap: 20px; }
    .board { order: -1; }
    .ledger { grid-template-columns: 1fr; }
    .ledger article, .ledger article:nth-child(even) { padding: 28px 0; border-left: 0; }
    .steps { grid-template-columns: 1fr; gap: 56px; }
    .steps li, .steps li:first-child { padding: 0; border-left: 0; }
    .steps .art { height: 160px; }
    .plans { grid-template-columns: 1fr; }
    .plan + .plan { border-left: 0; border-top: 1px solid var(--ring-soft); }
    .faq-grid { gap: 28px; }
    .closing-art { width: min(70%, 260px); order: -1; justify-self: start; }
    .foot-grid { grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr; }
    .foot-grid > div:first-child { grid-column: 1 / -1; }
  }
  @media (max-width: 860px) {
    .menu { display: none; }
    .menu-btn { display: block; }
    .site-header.menu-open { background: var(--night); }
    .site-header.menu-open .menu {
      display: flex; flex-direction: column; position: absolute; top: 76px; left: 0; right: 0;
      padding: 8px var(--gutter) 28px; background: var(--night); border-bottom: 1px solid var(--ring-soft);
      max-height: calc(100vh - 76px); overflow-y: auto;
    }
    .site-header.menu-open .nav { flex-direction: column; gap: 0; margin: 0; }
    .site-header.menu-open .nav a { padding: 14px 0; font-size: 1.15rem; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--ring-soft); }
    .site-header.menu-open .nav a::after { display: none; }
    .site-header.menu-open .bar-actions { flex-direction: column; margin-top: 20px; }
    .site-header.menu-open .bar-actions .btn { display: inline-flex; --h: 46px; }
  }
  @media (max-width: 520px) {
    body { font-size: 16px; }
    .signup { flex-direction: column; }
    .signup .btn { width: 100%; }
    .readout dd { font-size: 1.35rem; }
    .plan { padding: 32px 26px; }
  }
</style>
</head>
<body>
<a class="sr-only" href="#main">Skip to content</a>

<header class="site-header" data-section="nav">
  <div class="wrap bar">
    <a class="brand" href="#top" aria-label="Corelane home">
      <span class="brand-mark" aria-hidden="true"><i></i><i></i><i></i><i></i><i></i><i></i><i></i><i></i><i></i></span>
      Corelane
    </a>
    <div class="menu" id="menu">
    <nav class="nav" aria-label="Primary">
      <a href="#product">Product</a>
      <a href="#fleet">Live fleet</a>
      <a href="#how">How it works</a>
      <a href="#pricing">Pricing</a>
      <a href="#faq">FAQ</a>
    </nav>
    <div class="bar-actions">
      <a class="btn btn-sm" href="#signin">Sign in</a>
      <a class="btn btn-sm btn-fill" href="#start">Start free trial</a>
    </div>
    </div>
    <button class="menu-btn" type="button" aria-expanded="false" aria-controls="menu" aria-label="Open menu"><span></span><span></span></button>
  </div>
</header>

<main id="main">
  <section class="hero" id="top" data-section="hero">
    <div class="wrap hero-grid">
      <div class="hero-copy">
        <h1>Your team's laptops are already a build cluster</h1>
        <p class="lead">Corelane pools the idle cores on every machine in your office with one quiet on-site server. Builds and test suites finish in minutes, on hardware you already own, without your code leaving the building.</p>
        <div class="hero-ctas">
          <a class="btn btn-fill" href="#start">Start free trial</a>
          <a class="btn" href="#how">See how it works</a>
        </div>
        <p class="hero-note">Free for 14 days. Runs on macOS, Linux and Windows.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="hero-visual">
        <div class="stage" data-scene="1">

        </div>
        <div class="art-controls">
          <div class="segmented" role="group" aria-label="Illustration view">
            <button type="button" data-scene="1" aria-pressed="true">Team machines</button>
            <button type="button" data-scene="2" aria-pressed="false">Core server</button>
          </div>
          <span class="art-hint">Tip: click the server to lift it</span>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </section>

  <section class="section" id="product" data-section="product">
    <div class="wrap">
      <div class="section-head">
        <h2>Spare cycles, put to work</h2>
        <p class="lead">Most developer laptops sit near idle for most of the day. Corelane gives that capacity back to the whole team, and steps aside the moment its owner needs it.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="ledger">
        <article>
          <svg class="glyph" viewBox="0 0 46 46" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M23 5 41 15.5 23 26 5 15.5Z" fill="#e9e9fd"/><path d="M5 15.5V30l18 10.5V26Z" fill="#8f8ae0"/><path d="M41 15.5V30L23 40.5V26Z" fill="#5d58be"/><path d="M15 15.5 23 11l8 4.5-8 4.6Z" fill="#42e8e0"/></svg>
          <div><h3>Borrows only idle cores</h3><p>The agent works when a laptop is plugged in and quiet. Start typing, join a call or unplug, and its jobs move to another machine within seconds.</p></div>
        </article>
        <article>
          <svg class="glyph" viewBox="0 0 46 46" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M23 4 40 14 23 24 6 14Z" fill="#e9e9fd"/><path d="M23 13 40 23 23 33 6 23Z" fill="none" stroke="#42e8e0" stroke-width="1.5"/><path d="M23 22 40 32 23 42 6 32Z" fill="none" stroke="#706ec4" stroke-width="1.5"/></svg>
          <div><h3>Compiles each module once</h3><p>Build outputs land in a shared cache on the core server. When a teammate has already built a change, you download the result instead of rebuilding it.</p></div>
        </article>
        <article>
          <svg class="glyph" viewBox="0 0 46 46" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M23 5 41 15.5 23 26 5 15.5Z" fill="none" stroke="#706ec4" stroke-width="1.5"/><path d="M23 12 32 17 23 22 14 17Z" fill="#e84298"/><path d="M5 15.5V30l18 10.5L41 30V15.5" fill="none" stroke="#706ec4" stroke-width="1.5"/></svg>
          <div><h3>Keeps source on your network</h3><p>Code and artifacts stay inside your office. The core server only reaches out to fetch signed updates, and you can switch that off.</p></div>
        </article>
        <article>
          <svg class="glyph" viewBox="0 0 46 46" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M8 18h12v12H8z" transform="rotate(45 14 24)" fill="#42e8e0"/><path d="M26 18h12v12H26z" transform="rotate(45 32 24)" fill="#e9e9fd"/><path d="M20 24h6" stroke="#706ec4" stroke-width="2"/></svg>
          <div><h3>Fits the build you already have</h3><p>Works with Make, Bazel, Gradle, Cargo and most CI runners. You change one line of configuration, not your build scripts.</p></div>
        </article>
      </div>
    </div>
  </section>

  <section class="section fleet" id="fleet" data-section="fleet" aria-labelledby="fleet-title">
    <div class="wrap fleet-grid">
      <div class="fleet-copy">
        <h2 id="fleet-title">Every machine on the team, one queue</h2>
        <p class="lead">This is a 96-person office. Click a machine to send it to sleep or wake it up, then run a build and watch the work spread across whatever is available.</p>
        <dl class="readout">
          <div><dt>Machines available</dt><dd><span id="avail">0</span><small>of 96</small></dd></div>
          <div><dt>Full build on one laptop</dt><dd>41m 00s</dd></div>
          <div><dt>Full build with Corelane</dt><dd id="estimate">0m 00s</dd></div>
        </dl>
        <div class="fleet-actions">
          <button class="btn btn-fill" type="button" id="run">Run a build</button>
          <ul class="legend" aria-label="Legend">
            <li><i style="background:var(--cyan)"></i>Available</li>
            <li><i style="background:var(--magenta)"></i>Building</li>
            <li><i style="background:#4a3ca0"></i>Asleep</li>
          </ul>
        </div>
        <p class="sr-only" id="fleet-status" aria-live="polite"></p>
      </div>
      <div class="board">
        <span class="demo-tag">Interactive demo</span>
        <div class="plate"><div class="well" id="well" role="group" aria-label="Machines in the office. Use arrow keys to move, Enter to toggle sleep."></div></div>
        <span class="core-chip">Each square is one laptop or workstation</span>
      </div>
    </div>
  </section>

  <section class="section" id="how" data-section="how">
    <div class="wrap">
      <div class="section-head">
        <h2>Up and running in an afternoon</h2>
        <p class="lead">No new build system and no cloud account to provision. Three steps, and the second one is plugging something in.</p>
      </div>
      <ol class="steps">
        <li>

          <h3>Install the agent on each laptop</h3>
          <p>One command per machine, or push it with your device manager. Each laptop joins the fleet within a minute and shows up in the dashboard.</p>
        </li>
        <li>

          <h3>Plug in the Corelane core</h3>
          <p>A fanless box about the size of a hardcover book. It holds the shared cache and decides which machine runs which job.</p>
        </li>

          <h3>Point your builds at it</h3>
          <p>Add <code>remote = corelane.local</code> to your build config. Local builds, CI and test runs all start using the fleet.</p>
        </li>
      </ol>
    </div>
  </section>

  <section class="section pricing" id="pricing" data-section="pricing">
    <div class="wrap">
      <div class="section-head">
        <div>
          <h2>Priced per machine, not per minute</h2>
          <div class="billing">
            <span id="lbl-monthly">Monthly</span>
            <button class="switch" type="button" role="switch" aria-checked="true" aria-labelledby="lbl-annual" id="billing"></button>
            <span id="lbl-annual">Yearly <span class="save">(save 20%)</span></span>
          </div>
        </div>
        <p class="lead">Every plan includes the full scheduler and shared cache. You pay for the machines in your fleet, however many builds they run.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="plans">
        <div class="plan">
          <h3>Studio</h3>
          <p class="plan-desc">For small teams trying it out. One of your own machines acts as the core.</p>
          <p class="price"><span>$0</span></p>
          <p class="unit">Free forever, up to 8 machines</p>
          <ul>
            <li>Up to 8 machines</li>
            <li>50 GB shared cache</li>
            <li>Make, Bazel, Gradle and Cargo</li>
            <li>Community support</li>
          </ul>
          <a class="btn" href="#start">Start for free</a>
        </div>
        <div class="plan featured">
          <h3>Team <span class="badge">Most teams pick this</span></h3>
          <p class="plan-desc">For engineering teams who want builds off their laptops' critical path.</p>
          <p class="price"><span data-monthly="$24" data-annual="$19">$19</span></p>
          <p class="unit" data-monthly="per machine, per month, billed monthly" data-annual="per machine, per month, billed yearly">per machine, per month, billed yearly</p>
          <ul>
            <li>Up to 250 machines</li>
            <li>Corelane core server included</li>
            <li>2 TB shared cache</li>
            <li>CI runner integration</li>
            <li>Support within one business day</li>
          </ul>
          <a class="btn" href="#start">Start 14-day trial</a>
        </div>
        <div class="plan">
          <h3>Campus</h3>
          <p class="plan-desc">For organizations with several offices or strict compliance needs.</p>
          <p class="price"><span>Custom</span></p>
          <p class="unit">Volume pricing for 250+ machines</p>
          <ul>
            <li>Unlimited machines and offices</li>
            <li>Multiple linked cores</li>
            <li>Single sign-on and audit logs</li>
            <li>On-site installation</li>
          </ul>
          <a class="btn" href="#contact">Talk to sales</a>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </section>

  <section class="section" id="faq" data-section="faq">
    <div class="wrap faq-grid">
      <div>
        <h2>Questions teams ask first</h2>
        <p class="lead">Something else on your mind? <a href="#contact">Write to us</a> and an engineer will reply.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="faq">
        <details>
          <summary>Will it slow down my laptop?</summary>
          <p>No. The agent only accepts work while your machine is idle and plugged in. As soon as you use the keyboard, open a call or switch to battery, it finishes or hands off its current job and stops taking new ones.</p>
        </details>
        <details>
          <summary>What happens if a laptop sleeps in the middle of a job?</summary>
          <p>The core notices within a couple of seconds and reruns that job on another machine. Builds are split into small, repeatable steps, so nothing is lost but those few seconds.</p>
        </details>
        <details>
          <summary>Is our source code safe on other people's machines?</summary>
          <p>Jobs run in a sandbox that is wiped afterwards, and all traffic between machines is encrypted. Nothing is stored on a teammate's disk after a job finishes, and nothing leaves your network.</p>
        </details>
        <details>
          <summary>Do we need the core server?</summary>
          <p>Teams of up to eight can run one of their own machines as the core on the Studio plan. Past that, the dedicated core keeps the cache fast and the schedule steady, and it's included with Team.</p>
        </details>
        <details>
          <summary>Which build tools and platforms do you support?</summary>
          <p>The agent runs on macOS 13+, Ubuntu 22.04+ and Windows 11. Make, Bazel, Gradle and Cargo work out of the box, and anything that supports remote execution can be connected.</p>
        </details>
      </div>
    </div>
  </section>

  // ---------- object model ----------
  const OBJ = {};
  const dirtyNodes = new Set(), dirtyShapes = new Set(), dirtyColors = new Set();
  for (const id in D.nodes) {
    const [x, y, rotation, scaleX, scaleY, opacity] = D.nodes[id];
    OBJ[id] = { kind: 'node', el: document.getElementById('n' + id), x, y, rotation, scaleX, scaleY, opacity };
  }
  for (const id in D.colors) {
    const [elId, attr, op] = D.colors[id];
    OBJ[id] = { kind: 'color', el: document.getElementById(elId), attr, op, colorValue: 0 };
  }
  for (const id in D.dyn) {
    const [paths, els] = D.dyn[id];
    const shape = { els: els.map(e => document.getElementById(e)), paths: [] };
    for (const [pid, [x, y, rotation, scaleX, scaleY], closed, verts] of paths) {
      const p = OBJ[pid] = { kind: 'path', shape: id, x, y, rotation, scaleX, scaleY, closed, verts: [] };
      for (const v of verts) {
        const o = v[1] === 0
          ? { kind: 'vertex', shape: id, cubic: false, x: v[2], y: v[3], radius: v[4] }
          : { kind: 'vertex', shape: id, cubic: true, x: v[2], y: v[3], inRotation: v[4], inDistance: v[5], outRotation: v[6], outDistance: v[7] };
        OBJ[v[0]] = o; p.verts.push(o);
      }
      shape.paths.push(p);
    }
    OBJ['s' + id] = shape;
  }

  function setProp(id, prop, value) {
    const o = OBJ[id]; if (!o || o[prop] === value) return;
    o[prop] = value;
    if (o.kind === 'node') dirtyNodes.add(o);
    else if (o.kind === 'color') dirtyColors.add(o);
    else dirtyShapes.add(o.shape);
  }

  const f = n => Math.round(n * 1000) / 1000;
  function matrix(o) {
    const c = Math.cos(o.rotation), s = Math.sin(o.rotation);
    return [c * o.scaleX, s * o.scaleX, -s * o.scaleY, c * o.scaleY, o.x, o.y];
  }
  const apply = (m, x, y) => [m[0] * x + m[2] * y + m[4], m[1] * x + m[3] * y + m[5]];

  // Port of the vector path construction (straight / rounded / detached-cubic vertices).
  const IARC = 1 - 0.5522847498;
  function corner(pos, prev, next, radius) {
    let px = prev[0] - pos[0], py = prev[1] - pos[1], lp = Math.hypot(px, py) || 1e-9;
    let nx = next[0] - pos[0], ny = next[1] - pos[1], ln = Math.hypot(nx, ny) || 1e-9;
    px /= lp; py /= lp; nx /= ln; ny /= ln;
    const r = Math.min(lp / 2, ln / 2, radius);
    return { a: [pos[0] + px * r, pos[1] + py * r], b: [pos[0] + px * IARC * r, pos[1] + py * IARC * r],
             c: [pos[0] + nx * IARC * r, pos[1] + ny * IARC * r], end: [pos[0] + nx * r, pos[1] + ny * r] };
  }
  function pathD(p) {
    const V = p.verts.map(v => v.cubic
      ? { cubic: true, pt: [v.x, v.y], inP: [v.x + Math.cos(v.inRotation) * v.inDistance, v.y + Math.sin(v.inRotation) * v.inDistance],
          outP: [v.x + Math.cos(v.outRotation) * v.outDistance, v.y + Math.sin(v.outRotation) * v.outDistance] }
      : { cubic: false, pt: [v.x, v.y], inP: [v.x, v.y], outP: [v.x, v.y], radius: v.radius || 0 });
    const n = V.length; if (n < 2) return '';
    const m = matrix(p), P = q => { const r = apply(m, q[0], q[1]); return f(r[0]) + ' ' + f(r[1]); };
    let d = '', prevOut, prevCubic, startPt;
    const first = V[0], roundStart = !first.cubic && first.radius > 0 && p.closed;
    if (roundStart) {
      const k = corner(first.pt, V[n - 1].outP, V[1].inP, first.radius);
      d += 'M' + P(k.a) + 'C' + P(k.b) + ' ' + P(k.c) + ' ' + P(k.end);
      prevOut = k.end; prevCubic = false; startPt = k.a;
    } else {
      d += 'M' + P(first.pt); prevOut = first.outP; prevCubic = first.cubic; startPt = first.pt;
    }
    const seq = []; for (let i = 1; i < n; i++) seq.push(i); if (p.closed) seq.push(0);
    for (const i of seq) {
      const v = V[i];
      if (i === 0 && roundStart) { d += prevCubic ? 'C' + P(prevOut) + ' ' + P(startPt) + ' ' + P(startPt) : 'L' + P(startPt); break; }
      if (v.cubic) { d += 'C' + P(prevOut) + ' ' + P(v.inP) + ' ' + P(v.pt); prevOut = v.outP; prevCubic = true; continue; }
      if (v.radius > 0 && (p.closed || (i > 0 && i < n - 1))) {
        const k = corner(v.pt, V[i - 1].outP, V[(i + 1) % n].inP, v.radius);
        d += prevCubic ? 'C' + P(prevOut) + ' ' + P(k.a) + ' ' + P(k.a) : 'L' + P(k.a);
        d += 'C' + P(k.b) + ' ' + P(k.c) + ' ' + P(k.end); prevOut = k.end;
      } else {
        d += prevCubic ? 'C' + P(prevOut) + ' ' + P(v.pt) + ' ' + P(v.pt) : 'L' + P(v.pt); prevOut = v.pt;
      }
      prevCubic = false;
    }
    return p.closed ? d + 'Z' : d;
  }

  function flush() {
    for (const o of dirtyNodes) {
      if (!o.el) continue;
      o.el.setAttribute('transform', 'matrix(' + matrix(o).map(f).join(' ') + ')');
      o.el.setAttribute('opacity', f(Math.max(0, Math.min(1, o.opacity))));
    }
    for (const o of dirtyColors) {
      if (!o.el) continue;
      const v = o.colorValue >>> 0, a = ((v >>> 24) & 255) / 255;
      o.el.setAttribute(o.attr, '#' + (v & 0xffffff).toString(16).padStart(6, '0'));
      o.el.setAttribute(o.attr + '-opacity', f(a * o.op));
    }
    for (const id of dirtyShapes) {
      const s = OBJ['s' + id], d = s.paths.map(pathD).join('');
      for (const el of s.els) el && el.setAttribute('d', d);
    }
    dirtyNodes.clear(); dirtyColors.clear(); dirtyShapes.clear();
  }

  // ---------- keyframe animation ----------
  const [X1, Y1, X2, Y2] = D.ease;
  function bez(t, a, b) { return 3 * a * t * (1 - t) * (1 - t) + 3 * b * t * t * (1 - t) + t * t * t; }
  function ease(x) {
    let lo = 0, hi = 1, t = x;
    for (let i = 0; i < 24; i++) { t = (lo + hi) / 2; if (bez(t, X1, X2) < x) lo = t; else hi = t; }
    return bez(t, Y1, Y2);
  }
  function lerpColor(a, b, t) {
    let out = 0;
    for (let sh = 0; sh < 32; sh += 8) {
      const ca = (a >>> sh) & 255, cb = (b >>> sh) & 255;
      out += Math.round(ca + (cb - ca) * t) * 2 ** sh;
    }
    return out;
  }
  function sample(prop, kfs, fps, time) {
    const isColor = prop === 'colorValue';
    if (time <= kfs[0][0] / fps) return kfs[0][1];
    for (let i = 0; i < kfs.length - 1; i++) {
      const a = kfs[i], b = kfs[i + 1], ta = a[0] / fps, tb = b[0] / fps;
      if (time < tb) {
        if (a[2] === 0) return a[1];
        let t = (time - ta) / (tb - ta);
        if (a[2] === 2) t = ease(t);
        return isColor ? lerpColor(a[1], b[1], t) : a[1] + (b[1] - a[1]) * t;
      }
    }
    return kfs[kfs.length - 1][1];
  }
  function applyAnim(name, time) {
    const A = D.anims[name];
    for (const [id, prop, kfs] of A.keys) setProp(id, prop, sample(prop, kfs, A.fps, time));
  }
  // A playing instance: oneShot (0), loop (1) or pingPong (2), optionally reversed.
  function Instance(name, dirSpeed = 1) {
    const A = D.anims[name], end = A.dur / A.fps;
    const inst = { name, time: dirSpeed < 0 ? end : 0, dir: 1, done: false };
    inst.advance = dt => {
      inst.time += dt * A.speed * dirSpeed * inst.dir;
      if (A.loop === 1) { inst.time = ((inst.time % end) + end) % end; }
      else if (A.loop === 2) {
        while (inst.time > end || inst.time < 0) {
          if (inst.time > end) { inst.time = 2 * end - inst.time; inst.dir *= -1; }
          else { inst.time = -inst.time; inst.dir *= -1; }
        }
      } else {
        if (inst.time >= end) { inst.time = end; inst.done = true; }
        if (inst.time <= 0 && dirSpeed < 0) { inst.time = 0; inst.done = true; }
      }
    };
    inst.apply = () => applyAnim(name, inst.time);
    return inst;
  }

  // ---------- state machine (4 layers, same logic as the original) ----------
  const input = { pages: 1, up: false, signin: false, learn: false };
  const layers = [
    { // Layer 1 – which illustration plays
      state: null,
      pick() {
        const want = (input.pages === 2 || input.pages === 4) ? 'server' : (input.pages === 1 || input.pages === 3) ? 'laptops' : null;
        if (this.state !== want) { this.state = want; this.inst = want && Instance(want); }
      }
    },
    { // Layer 2 – navigation state (scene scale/visibility + underline)
      state: null,
      pick() {
        const want = ['about', 'pricing', 'contacts', 'partners'][input.pages - 1] || null;
        if (this.state !== want) { this.state = want; this.inst = want && Instance(want); }
      }
    },
    { // Layer 3 – button press pulses (triggers)
      state: null,
      pick() {
        if (this.state && this.inst.done) { this.state = null; this.inst = null; }
        if (!this.state) {
          if (input.signin) { this.state = 'sign in'; this.inst = Instance('sign in'); }
          else if (input.learn) { this.state = 'learn more'; this.inst = Instance('learn more'); }
        }
      }
    },
    { // Layer 4 – click the central server to raise / lower it
      state: null,
      pick() {
        if (this.state === null && input.up) { this.state = 'fwd'; this.inst = Instance('up', 1); }
        else if (this.state === 'fwd' && !input.up && this.inst.done) { this.state = 'rev'; this.inst = Instance('up', -1); }
        else if (this.state === 'rev' && input.up && this.inst.done) { this.state = 'fwd'; this.inst = Instance('up', 1); }
      }
    }
  ];

  function step(dt) {
    for (const L of layers) {
      L.pick();
      if (L.inst) { L.inst.advance(dt); L.inst.apply(); }
    }
    input.signin = input.learn = false; // triggers are consumed each frame
    flush();
  }

  // ---------- hero controls ----------
  const reduce = matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
  const sceneSvg = document.querySelector('.hero-art');
  const switches = document.querySelectorAll('[data-scene]');
  const hint = document.querySelector('.art-hint');
  const stage = sceneSvg.closest('.stage');
  const circuits = stage.querySelector('svg.circuits');
  if (reduce) stage.querySelectorAll('animate.motion').forEach(a => a.remove());
  switches.forEach(btn => btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
    const page = +btn.dataset.scene;
    if (page === input.pages) return;
    switches.forEach(b => b.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(b === btn)));
    if (hint) hint.hidden = page !== 1;
    const swap = () => {
      input.pages = page; if (page !== 1) input.up = false; step(0); if (reduce) step(5);
      stage.dataset.scene = String(page);
      if (circuits && circuits.pauseAnimations) page === 1 ? circuits.unpauseAnimations() : circuits.pauseAnimations();
    };
    if (reduce) { swap(); return; }
    sceneSvg.classList.add('is-swapping');
    setTimeout(() => { swap(); sceneSvg.classList.remove('is-swapping'); }, 220);
  }));
  const server = document.getElementById('n1768');
  if (server) {
    server.setAttribute('tabindex', '0');
    server.setAttribute('role', 'button');
    server.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Lift or lower the core server');
    const toggle = () => { input.up = !input.up; if (reduce) { step(0); step(5); } };
    server.addEventListener('click', toggle);
    server.addEventListener('keydown', e => { if (e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' ') { e.preventDefault(); toggle(); } });
  }

  step(0);
  if (reduce) return;
  let last = performance.now(), visible = true, raf = 0;
  function frame(now) {
    const dt = Math.min(0.1, (now - last) / 1000); last = now;
    step(dt);
    raf = visible ? requestAnimationFrame(frame) : 0;
  }
  new IntersectionObserver(([en]) => {
    visible = en.isIntersecting;
    if (visible && !raf) { last = performance.now(); raf = requestAnimationFrame(frame); }
  }).observe(sceneSvg);
  raf = requestAnimationFrame(frame);
})();

</script>
<script>
(() => {
  const reduce = matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;

  /* header: background after scroll, mobile menu, current section */
  const header = document.querySelector('.site-header');
  const onScroll = () => header.classList.toggle('is-scrolled', scrollY > 12);
  addEventListener('scroll', onScroll, { passive: true }); onScroll();
  const menuBtn = document.querySelector('.menu-btn');
  const setMenu = open => {
    header.classList.toggle('menu-open', open);
    menuBtn.setAttribute('aria-expanded', String(open));
    menuBtn.setAttribute('aria-label', open ? 'Close menu' : 'Open menu');
  };
  menuBtn.addEventListener('click', () => setMenu(menuBtn.getAttribute('aria-expanded') !== 'true'));
  header.querySelectorAll('.nav a, .bar-actions a').forEach(a => a.addEventListener('click', () => setMenu(false)));
  addEventListener('keydown', e => { if (e.key === 'Escape') setMenu(false); });
  const navLinks = [...document.querySelectorAll('.nav a')];
  const spy = new IntersectionObserver(entries => {
    entries.forEach(en => {
      if (!en.isIntersecting) return;
      navLinks.forEach(a => a.classList.toggle('is-current', a.hash === '#' + en.target.id));
    });
  }, { rootMargin: '-45% 0px -50% 0px' });
  navLinks.forEach(a => { const s = document.querySelector(a.hash); if (s) spy.observe(s); });

  /* fleet demo */
  const well = document.getElementById('well'), COLS = 12, ROWS = 8, N = COLS * ROWS;
  const availEl = document.getElementById('avail'), estEl = document.getElementById('estimate');
  const runBtn = document.getElementById('run'), status = document.getElementById('fleet-status');
  const SINGLE = 41 * 60;
  const asleepStart = new Set([5, 17, 30, 44, 51, 63, 70, 82, 91]);
  const cells = [];
  let running = false, focusIdx = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) {
    const b = document.createElement('button');
    b.type = 'button'; b.className = 'cell' + (asleepStart.has(i) ? ' asleep' : '');
    b.tabIndex = i === 0 ? 0 : -1;
    b.addEventListener('click', () => { if (running) return; b.classList.toggle('asleep'); label(i); update(); });
    b.addEventListener('focus', () => { cells[focusIdx].tabIndex = -1; focusIdx = i; b.tabIndex = 0; });
    well.appendChild(b); cells.push(b); label(i);
  }
  function label(i) {
    const asleep = cells[i].classList.contains('asleep'), busy = cells[i].classList.contains('busy');
    cells[i].setAttribute('aria-label', `Machine ${i + 1}, ${busy ? 'building' : asleep ? 'asleep' : 'available'}`);
  }
  well.addEventListener('keydown', e => {
    const moves = { ArrowRight: 1, ArrowLeft: -1, ArrowDown: COLS, ArrowUp: -COLS };
    if (!(e.key in moves)) return;
    e.preventDefault();
    const next = focusIdx + moves[e.key];
    if (next >= 0 && next < N) cells[next].focus();
  });
  const fmt = s => `${Math.floor(s / 60)}m ${String(Math.round(s % 60)).padStart(2, '0')}s`;
  const available = () => cells.filter(c => !c.classList.contains('asleep'));
  // Parallel speed-up with diminishing returns: the more machines, the smaller each one's share.
  const estimate = n => n ? SINGLE / (1 + 0.16 * (n - 1)) : SINGLE;
  function update() {
    const n = available().length;
    availEl.textContent = n;
    estEl.textContent = fmt(estimate(n));
    runBtn.disabled = n === 0;
  }
  update();
  runBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
    if (running) return;
    const pool = available(); if (!pool.length) return;
    running = true; runBtn.disabled = true; runBtn.textContent = 'Building…';
    const target = estimate(pool.length);
    status.textContent = `Build started on ${pool.length} machines.`;
    if (reduce) {
      estEl.textContent = fmt(target);
      finish(pool, target); return;
    }
    // spread work outward from the centre of the office, then finish at staggered times
    const cx = (COLS - 1) / 2, cy = (ROWS - 1) / 2, dur = 2600;
    pool.forEach(c => {
      const i = cells.indexOf(c), d = Math.hypot(i % COLS - cx, Math.floor(i / COLS) - cy);
      setTimeout(() => { c.classList.add('busy'); label(i); }, d * 70);
      setTimeout(() => { c.classList.remove('busy'); label(i); }, 900 + d * 70 + Math.random() * 1500);
    });
    const t0 = performance.now();
    (function tick(now) {
      const p = Math.min(1, (now - t0) / dur);
      estEl.textContent = fmt(target * p);
      if (p < 1) requestAnimationFrame(tick); else finish(pool, target);
    })(t0);
  });
  function finish(pool, target) {
    setTimeout(() => {
      pool.forEach(c => { c.classList.remove('busy'); label(cells.indexOf(c)); });
      running = false; runBtn.textContent = 'Run it again'; runBtn.disabled = false;
      estEl.textContent = fmt(target);
      status.textContent = `Build finished in ${fmt(target)} across ${pool.length} machines.`;
    }, reduce ? 0 : 500);
  }

  /* pricing toggle */
  const billing = document.getElementById('billing');
  const setBilling = annual => {
    billing.setAttribute('aria-checked', String(annual));
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-annual]').forEach(el => { el.textContent = annual ? el.dataset.annual : el.dataset.monthly; });
  };
  billing.addEventListener('click', () => setBilling(billing.getAttribute('aria-checked') !== 'true'));
  document.getElementById('lbl-monthly').addEventListener('click', () => setBilling(false));
  document.getElementById('lbl-annual').addEventListener('click', () => setBilling(true));

  /* trial form (connect to your own endpoint) */
  const form = document.getElementById('signup'), msg = document.getElementById('form-msg');
  form.addEventListener('submit', e => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const email = form.email.value.trim();
    if (!/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(email)) {
      msg.className = 'form-msg err';
      msg.textContent = 'Enter a work email like you@company.com.';
      form.email.focus(); return;
    }
    msg.className = 'form-msg ok';
    msg.textContent = `Trial started. We sent the install command to ${email}.`;
    form.reset();
  });
})();
</script>
</body>
</html>
```
